**This is the ONLY open-work list** (owner, 2026-09-12). Handoff memories, the
audit-bus ledger and agent reports are evidence, not backlogs: anything still
open from them gets a line here, with the check that guards it once one exists.

**Layout (reconciled 2026-09-23, Q16):** (1) the live QUEUE, verbatim, first;
(2) every item still open from older sections, one line each with its current
status and a pointer into the archive; (3) the 2026-09-02 ledger's leftovers
(docs/audit/OPEN_ITEMS.md is retired to a pointer); (4) the audit bus, by
reference. History (closed sections, narratives) is verbatim in
[docs/archive/OPEN-history-2026-09.md](archive/OPEN-history-2026-09.md),
with a reconcile log of every item closed and its evidence. Nothing was deleted.

**Done items leave this file (Q16, 2026-09-26):** a ticked `- [x]` item is moved
verbatim to the dated archive docs/archive/OPEN-done-YYYY-MM.md by
`npm run inventories:refresh` (scripts/archive-done.mjs); check:generated fails
while one is left here. The queue score, next free number and the done-item
guard check read this file AND the archives, so archiving never changes a count.

**Priority among the open queue items (lead's order, 2026-09-23 — money and
security first, then alerting, then the audit, then hygiene):** Q53 (DB outage
root cause), Q50 (card holds on cancelled jobs, Stripe side), Q3 (Stripe test
balance + monitor), Q30 (missed daily crons never catch up), Q2 (test runs raise
real alerts), Q42 (open alert-ledger items), Q9 (skipped money/authz review),
Q14 (Supabase security advisors), Q45 (prove a restore), Q58 (one place
for everything open), Q52 (false-green hunt), Q54 (front/back parity), Q35 (exhaustive
audit), Q44 (partial gates turn main red), Q11/Q15/Q39 (Sentry + error
screens), Q10 (owner-side), then the rest in number order. An item's own line
is the source of truth for its state; this sentence only orders them.

- [ ] **Q7 WebKit only: the bottom nav isn't frosted. OWNER (device), re-checked 2026-09-26: still unmeasured in WebKit.** Nothing code-side can settle it. The dock is signed-in only (guest /browse renders no MobileNav, checked at 375), the cloud session that took this item had no test-account credentials, and Playwright WebKit on Linux is not the iOS compositor, so no CI run can show WKWebView frost either. What the source does (MobileNav.tsx:705-745): the full-width curtain carries `backdropFilter`/`WebkitBackdropFilter: blur(32px) saturate(170%)` together with `maskImage`/`WebkitMaskImage` on the SAME element, and the pill carries `blur(40px) saturate(180%)` inside the nav, whose own style has `transform` + `willChange: transform` (the hide-on-scroll slide). Both are inline styles, so the CSS minifier cannot have dropped the -webkit- form. **Ask:** on the iOS 26.1 simulator or a device, signed in, open /home, scroll the feed under the dock and send one screenshot. **Recommendation:** if the pill is frosted and only the band above it is clear, the curtain's mask is the suspect (test by removing `WebkitMaskImage` alone). If the pill is clear too, test with `willChange` removed from the nav. Fix whichever one screenshot proves, and add a pixel-variance check under the dock to a WebKit spec.

5. **Push notifications (Q82): three things only you can do.** The code
   cause is fixed and pushed (see Q82), but no phone has the fix yet.
   (a) **Cut a TestFlight build from main** (at or after the Q82 fix commit)
   and install it on your iPhone. The only builds with the AppDelegate
   forwarding are ce4d38d59 (2026-09-02) and later, and every build so far
   also has the boot race (it depends on timing, so it may not hit every
   launch). (b) **On that build: sign in, go to Home, tap "Enable" on the
   notifications pill, then Allow.** The pill only shows after you've posted
   or applied, or on a later launch more than an hour after install. If you tapped "Don't
   Allow" on any earlier build, iOS will not ask again: turn it on in
   Settings > Helpr > Notifications first, then cold-launch the app. Within
   seconds `select count(*) from push_tokens` should be 1 and
   analytics_events should show `push_token_saved`. If it doesn't, the
   analytics events say which step failed. (c) **Only if you run the app
   straight from Xcode on your phone:** those builds get SANDBOX APNs tokens,
   and prod sends to the production APNs host (`APNS_USE_SANDBOX` is unset),
   so APNs rejects them with BadDeviceToken and the sender deletes the row.
   TestFlight/App Store builds are unaffected. Leave this alone unless you
   test from Xcode. APNs credentials are fine: APNS_KEY_ID, APNS_TEAM_ID
   (digest matches P85MCK558V), APNS_BUNDLE_ID (digest matches com.Helpr)
   and APNS_AUTH_KEY are all set in Supabase secrets. Whether the .p8 key
   itself is valid can't be checked without a real token to send to.

- [~] **Q77 FIXED 2026-09-23 (branch cloud/q77-q89-retry), live effect on the Mac not yet measured. Finished agents' worktrees pile up.** Every worktree-isolated agent
  leaves a LOCKED worktree under .claude/worktrees/, and prune-git-hygiene
  never touches locked ones (by design, since running agents lock theirs). Add a
  safe rule: a worktree whose agent has finished (its branch is merged into
  origin/main or has no commits, no process has cwd there, and it's older
  than 2h) is unlocked and removed; anything unmerged is reported, never deleted.
  DONE: rule in scripts/lib/worktreeHygiene.mjs, called by
  scripts/prune-git-hygiene.mjs. A LOCKED worktree under <main>/.claude/worktrees/
  is unlocked and removed (`git worktree remove`, never --force) when HEAD is in
  origin/main (rev-list 0, or every commit upstream by patch-id via `git cherry`),
  status is clean, no process cwd is inside (lsof; unknown = remove nothing), the
  pid in its lock reason (if any) is not running, and it is older than 2h. Unmerged
  or dirty ones are REPORTED in the hygiene output/log, never removed. Main is never
  a candidate; locks elsewhere are still skipped. GUARD:
  src/test/pruneAgentWorktrees.test.ts (fixture repo: bare origin + clone + 7 linked
  worktrees; plan AND apply; 11 @mutate, all killed, incl. restoring the old
  "skip every locked worktree" rule). TO TICK: the next session-start hygiene log
  (~/.lh-hygiene/hygiene.log) on the owner's Mac shows the .claude/worktrees count
  falling; the Claude Code lock-reason format (pid or not) was not observable here.

  'skipped: no_registered_devices' x3,532 across 87 users since 2026-09-01.
  The owner's iPhone (iOS 18.7, native) signed in 10x between 09-03 and 09-09
  on TestFlight build ce4d38d59, which already has the AppDelegate
  forwarding. No push error was ever logged. NOT "never held a row":
  notification_logs shows 2 token rows deleted on 09-01/02 after APNs
  BadDeviceToken. Their users have no profile, so they look like synthetic
  probes; real-device rows: unknown.
  LAYERS: native AppDelegate forwarding + entitlements + SPM plugin: correct
  (code read). Server: upsert/select/delete own row as helper-e2e via PostgREST
  201/200/200, another user's row 403 (RLS correct); no trigger or cron prunes
  rows (only purge_user_data, sign-out, and the sender on BadDeviceToken). APNs
  secrets present, bundle/team digests match. **CLIENT (the break):**
  useNativePushSetup's effect depended on `navigate`, whose identity changes
  on every pathname change in react-router 7. A native cold launch at "/" is
  redirected at once by NativeLaunchRouter, so the setup aborted before
  register() and before the appUrlOpen listener. That kills token registration,
  Universal Links and the helpr:/// Stripe return for the whole session. Also,
  sign-out followed by sign-in in the same session never re-saved the token.
  FIXED in src/lib/nativePush.ts. GUARDS: src/lib/nativePush.bootRegister.test.tsx
  (red 6/7 on the old code, green 7/7; 3 @mutate lines, all killed) +
  LIVE MONITOR migration 20260923055631 (check_push_token_health, daily cron
  push-token-health: ledger item 'push-tokens-empty' while no real user has a
  token, closed only by ops_alert_verify re-asking; PGlite proof
  src/test/pglite/pushTokenHealth.pglite.mjs). Stays [~] until a real device's
  row lands, which needs the owner (MORNING QUESTIONS 5). Scoreboard: Q59
  doesn't exist yet. When it does, it reads check_push_token_health()
  (tokens, registered_14d, native_users_14d, skipped_no_device_7d).

No native build has signed in for 14 days, and Q387 shows the one installed on the owner's iPhone predates months of fixes. 7. **Turn off Supabase's GitHub auto-deploy to production (Q121). Top priority.**
   On every push to main, Supabase's GitHub integration applies new migrations
   (and deploys edge functions) to prod within about a minute. Our deploy
   workflow's safety checks (lint, replay test, destructive-DDL stop) run after
   that, so for the last 24h (24 migrations) they have not blocked anything.
   Measured from prod's own logs 2026-09-23 (Q117). What you do: Supabase
   dashboard > Project Settings > Integrations > GitHub, and switch off
   automatic deploys to the production branch (exact toggle name not verified).
   Until then, each new migration turns db-deploy red by design (the new
   provenance check), so the side channel stays visible.

7. **Backups (Q45): the restore is proven weekly now; five things are yours.**
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-23 (owner pop-ups): (a) yes, Pro is paid and intended; (b) keep 14-day retention; (c) back up uploaded files EXCEPT id-documents, encrypted (work item Q147); (d) no PITR (no paid upgrade); (e) BACKUP_PASSPHRASE and ban_fingerprint_salt go in the owner's password manager (owner to-do).**
   Measured 2026-09-23. Nothing below is needed for the drill to stay green.
   (a) **Is the Supabase org really on Pro?** The API says `plan: "pro"`, and
   `supabase backups list` shows 7 daily platform backups. Every doc said
   "free tier". If you are paying $25/mo, you already have in-place daily
   restores for 7 days. If you did not mean to be on Pro, that is a bill.
   (b) **Backup retention is 14 days, not 90.** The repo caps artifacts and
   logs at 14 days (`maximum_allowed_days` 90). Raising it to 90 is one
   setting, but it lengthens EVERY workflow's artifacts, and on the free
   GitHub plan that can go past the artifact-storage quota (billed). The
   alternative is a monthly copy somewhere else. Your call.
   (c) **Storage files (photos, ID documents) are in NO backup**: 152 objects,
   about 10 MB (proof-photos 64, job-photos 61, message-attachments 18,
   user-documents 4, avatars 4, id-documents 1). The platform backup excludes
   them too. Backing them up needs a service-role key as an Actions secret and
   puts ID documents (encrypted) into a GitHub artifact: a privacy trade-off.
   (d) **PITR** (restore to the minute) is a paid add-on, about $100/mo plus
   Small compute. Without it, worst-case loss is about 24 h (platform) or
   24 h + GitHub's up-to-7 h schedule delay (ours). You said no paid upgrades,
   so this is only the measured gap.
   (e) **Two secrets that no backup holds.** `BACKUP_PASSPHRASE`: is there a
   copy outside GitHub? If not, losing the GitHub account also loses every
   backup. `ban_fingerprint_salt` (vault): it cannot be re-created, and
   without it every stored ban fingerprint stops matching after a restore.
   Want it (encrypted) in the backup, or kept in your password manager?

   **ANSWERED 2026-09-24 (owner pop-up): (a) the poster pays the card fee on top, and the tip minimum rises to $3. Work item Q362.**

   $1.34 (34% extra, which looks like a bug). Reply: (a) ship it and raise the tip

15. **Tax forms (W-9) after a Helpr deletes their account (CS-003, 2026-09-24).**
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-24 (owner pop-up): keep 4 years after signing, then delete automatically. Work item Q370.**
   Deleting an account leaves the Helpr's W-9 record (typed legal name, signing IP)
   in place forever. The IRS expects a business to keep W-9s for about 4 years, so
   deleting them at once may be wrong too. Options: (a) keep them 4 years after
   deletion, then delete automatically; (b) delete them with the account; (c) keep
   forever. I recommend (a), but it is a legal call. No W-9s exist in prod yet.

- [~] **Q295 /admin?view=credentials "Open" scored "no observable change (focus moved, nothing else)" (press run 35837735324, found by Q128).** **STATUS 2026-09-26 (cloud/open-ops): FIXED in code, live row not re-measured.** Root cause by code read: SignedOpenLink awaited createSignedUrl and only then called window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener'). WebKit (Safari and the iOS WKWebView) drops the click's user activation across the await and blocks the popup, and with noopener window.open returns null, so nothing opened and nothing was said. Now src/lib/openSignedDocument.ts opens the tab inside the click, nulls its opener, points it at the signed URL, closes it with a toast if signing fails, and toasts a refused popup; signing errors are reported. Guard: src/lib/openSignedDocument.test.ts (5 @mutate, each red). NOT measured: whether Chromium's press run saw the old popup at all (the item's other hypothesis, a popup after the after-snapshot); the press run's credentials row is the live proof. SignedOpenLink (AdminCredentialQueue.tsx) awaits createSignedUrl and then `window.open(…, "_blank", "noopener")`; the press listens for new pages on the context, so either the popup landed after the after-snapshot, the click lost user activation across the await, or the signed URL failed without a toast. Not measured (needs a live admin session). Guard to name when fixed: the press run's credentials row green, or a unit test on SignedOpenLink's outcome.

Decide: tag or drop sweep events (environment "press"/beforeSend on a test flag), or route them to a separate project. - [~] **Q307 FIXED on cloud/q307-q348 2026-09-26, lands with the PR (then [x]): every template now proves its EVENT from DB state, not only its sender.** supabase/functions/_shared/notification-templates.ts: work_started = job in_progress + the Helpr's job_tracking row "working"; dispute_withdrawn / dispute_resolved = newest disputes row withdrawn AND opened by the sender (rpc_withdraw_dispute lets only the opener close it); dispute_response = jobs.dispute_status live + dispute_helper_response (not the disputes row: 2 live jobs have an active dispute_status and no disputes row, measured 2026-09-26); revision_acknowledged = newest job_revisions row accepted (HelperRevisionCard no longer sends it on the legacy revision_note path, which records nothing: 2 such jobs live); job_confirmed = the SENDER's own stamp (poster_confirmed_at / helper_dayof_confirmed_at); arrival_confirmed / work_confirmed = poster_confirmed_arrival_at / poster_confirmed_working_at; job_offer = target application accepted. create-notification reads those columns and answers 500 (not 409) when a fact read errors. GUARD: src/test/edge/create-notification.test.ts "Q307" block — STATE table must name EXACTLY the NOTIFICATION_TEMPLATES keys; per template "state not reached → 409, nothing inserted" and "state reached → lands". Red on the pre-fix templates: 9/12 fail (exactly the 9 named here; the 3 already-proven stay green); vacuity 8/8 killed. **lh-authz-rls REVIEW-ONLY pass done 2026-09-26, fixes in the same PR:** F2 dispute_resolved (type payment, "Payment will be released") now also needs payment_status payout_pending/released (live job bb2c3732 had the dispute withdrawn by the poster with escrow still held); F3 revision_acknowledged now needs the revision requested_by = the poster (job_revisions RLS lets the assigned Helpr insert rows; all 26 live rows are by the poster); F5 one-conjunct cases (dispute withdrawn by the other party, released-escrow, job status for work_started, empty dispute response, poster side of job_confirmed) + an assertion that the disputes / job_tracking / job_revisions reads are scoped to the job (and the caller), since the edge mock does not match on filters. Vacuity 13/13 on the file; scoping assertion red by hand with the disputes job_id filter removed. Not fixed, by design (F4): proofs are durable state, so a true notice can be re-sent while the state holds, bounded by the rate limit — documented in the template header. Was: **Q307 Notification templates check WHO sends, not WHETHER the event happened (Q223 follow-on, 2026-09-23).** Only revision_requested, application_declined and no_show_reported read the database to prove their event (a job_revisions row, a rejected application, a no_show strike). The other nine are gated to the right side of the job but not to its state: the assigned Helpr can send "Dispute withdrawn" on a job with no dispute, and the poster can send "Dispute resolved ✓ … Payment will be released" (type payment) before resolving. The words are all server-built and true-shaped, so the harm is a misleading notice about the sender's own job, not a phishing primitive. Fix: a state predicate per template in supabase/functions/_shared/notification-templates.ts (dispute_status, arrival / working stamps, poster/helper confirmation stamps), or move each notice into the RPC that performs the transition. Guard: extend src/test/edge/create-notification.test.ts with one "state not reached → 409" case per template.

- [~] **Q333 A stale `?jobId=&userId=<deleted id>` message link opens an empty sendable thread (Q262 review, 2026-09-24).** Fixed 2026-09-26 (branch cloud/open-messaging-deleted): buildDeepLinkPlaceholder (loadConversations.ts) now asks the server when the profile is missing and the job exists — new RPC get_thread_counterparty_deleted(job, other) (20260926015040, SECURITY DEFINER, REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon; true only when the caller has a thread on the job AND the id has no auth.users row and no profile, so a banned/unverified person whose profile get_safe_profiles hides is NOT called deleted). Deleted -> placeholder has otherUserId null ("Former member", read-only DELETED_ACCOUNT_NOTICE); useMessagesData opens the inbox's existing deleted-account thread for that job instead when there is one. RPC error or PGRST202 -> old behaviour. lh-authz-rls REVIEW-ONLY (2026-09-26): no blocking defects; accepted and documented in the migration: the caller is scoped but _other cannot be (a deleted account leaves no row on the job), so a member can tell "account gone" from "exists but hidden" for a UUID they already hold, and an id that never existed opens a read-only "Former member" thread. Guard: messagesReceiverNullable "the deep-link fallback routes a missing profile to the deleted-account thread (Q333)" + loadConversations.test.tsx and useMessagesData.test.tsx Q333 cases (5 @mutate lines, each shown red). The client wrapper src/lib/userBlocks.ts areUsersBlocked has 0 callers (grep src + supabase/functions). Fix direction: inside the function, answer only when auth.uid() is one of the pair or is_server_context() (else false/raise), after proving every caller passes the caller as one argument; or drop the dead wrapper. - [~] **Q349 One real account's avatar object is stored `Cache-Control: no-cache` and nothing I read wrote it (2026-09-24, Q329 remainder).** storage.objects avatars/7f65ef12-…/avatar.jpg (real, non-seed, not the owner; signed up 2026-05-03), written 2026-09-20 21:52Z with owner_id NULL (service-role). complete-signup is the only edge uploader and runs at signup, not months later; scripts/audit/prod-seed.mjs touches only the helper test account. Find the writer (storage API logs for that minute), then decide with the owner whether to rewrite the object's metadata. Not touched: real user data. **2026-09-26 (cloud/open-perf-client): code side DONE, OWNER TO-DO below.** Found how an object gets `no-cache`: Storage stores a raw HTTP upload that sends no cache-control header as `no-cache`. storage-js never does that (it always sends `max-age=3600`), so complete-signup (storage-js, no cacheControl option, so max-age=3600) is not the writer. The repo had 5 raw uploads, 4 without the header, one sending the invalid "3600" (scripts/audit/prod-seed.mjs ×2, the message-attachments probe, the prod-audit harness, the privacy spec). None writes to `avatars` for a real user, so the Q349 writer is still NOT in this repo: an ad-hoc service-role call (owner_id NULL) on 2026-09-20 21:52Z. All 5 now send `cache-control: max-age=3600`. GUARD: src/test/rawStorageUploadsSetCacheControl.test.ts (every `x-upsert` header set also sends max-age; red on the old files with 5 sites listed, 2/2 @mutate killed). Not done from here: this container has no service role and the Supabase MCP failed to connect, so the storage logs and the object's metadata could not be read. **OWNER TO-DO (5 min):** Supabase Dashboard → Logs → Storage, 2026-09-20 21:50–21:55 UTC, path `avatars/7f65ef12-…/avatar.jpg`, and note the user agent / IP of the POST. **Recommendation:** leave the object as it is. It is one real person's photo, costs one extra download per view, and rewriting it means re-uploading that person's data with the service role. Close this once the writer is named; if the writer turns out to be a tool we still use, give it the same header.

- [ ] **Q444 A group job can be marked released while a roster member is unpaid (lh-money-escrow review of Q355 part 2, 2026-09-26, code read only, not reproduced).** supabase/functions/stripe-webhook/handlers/transferCreated.ts flips jobs.payment_status to 'released' for ANY transfer that has a payout_transfers row, whenever the job is payout_pending/escrow/released, with no roster check. process-scheduled-payouts deliberately keeps a group job payout_pending until every roster member has a pending/paid payout_transfers row (the allRosterPaid block). So when one member's transfer throws ('Scheduled payout failed'), another member's transfer.created webhook can release the job; the cron selects payment_status = 'payout_pending' and never retries the unpaid member. Q355 part 2's money-held rule counts that state as still owed, so the alert stays open, but the payout itself is stuck. Fix: give transferCreated the same roster test (only the transfer that completes the roster may flip a group job), with a PGlite/edge test red on the current handler. Needs a money-escrow review.

- [~] **Q380** (alert, nightly-red #1719 e2e-journeys, 2026-09-24): 02-marketplace hire-and-message fails at 1440 on the "slow" network profile. The poster presses Message Helpr, which goes to `/messages?jobId=<job>&userId=<helper>` (confirmed in the trace); openConvo fetched the thread's messages, but the page ended on the list at /messages?chat=1 with no reply box. Reads as a race that closes the placeholder thread when the network is slow. The same deep link opens the thread with a reply box for helpr-e2e-poster-0902 on prod at normal speed (checked 2026-09-24). Evidence: the run's artifacts and trace, copied to the session scratchpad. ROOT CAUSE (trace): not a race. The inbox load, a full confirming refetch and then the placeholder lookup ran in series; a just-hired thread has no messages, so the refetch can never return it, and the placeholder's jobs read was still pending at the 30s timeout. FIXED 2026-09-24: the placeholder is built alongside the refetch (src/pages/messages/useMessagesData.ts). GUARD: src/pages/messages/useMessagesData.test.tsx "builds the placeholder while the confirming refetch is still in flight" (@mutate proven). PENDING: an e2e-journeys run green on the slow profile closes #1719.
- [~] **Q383** (LOW, money, found during ME-006 2026-09-25, code read only): STATUS 2026-09-26 (cloud/open-security-money, c71f006e1): MEASURED in Stripe test mode, the account's default payment-method configuration offers affirm, afterpay_clearpay, klarna, cashapp, us_bank_account, amazon_pay and more beside card. FIXED: the tip Checkout Session now sets payment_method_types TIP_PAYMENT_METHOD_TYPES = [card, link] (_shared/tipFees.ts; Apple/Google Pay are card wallets and stay); a test-mode session of exactly that shape was created and expired. auto-tip-charge already charges only a saved card. GUARD src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts (tip session asserts [card, link]; @mutate red). OWNER QUESTION (the refund rule): should a tip ever be refunded? Recommendation: tips are final in the app (no code path refunds one today); an admin Dashboard refund of a tip must use reverse_transfer=true and refund_application_fee=true so the Helpr's share and the fee come back together, and we write that into the admin runbook. Original text: the tip fee covers Stripe's CARD rate (2.9% + 30c). Tip Checkout sessions set no `payment_method_types`, so any other method the account enables (the stripeFees.ts comment lists Klarna/Affirm/Afterpay at 5.99% + 30c) costs more than the fee collected and the platform pays the difference; the Helpr still gets the full tip. Also: no code path refunds a tip, and a Dashboard refund of a tip does not reverse the Helpr's transfer unless `reverse_transfer` is set. Decide: restrict tip sessions to `card`, and write the tip refund rule down.

Trigger chosen over RPC + column REVOKE because authenticated holds TABLE-level UPDATE on jobs, which a column REVOKE does not remove. (b) EVIDENCE_PATH_RE lost its /i; (c) the legacy signed-URL branch is pinned to `/storage/v1/object/sign/proof-photos/<uuid>/disputes/<uuid>/<file>` (src/lib/evidenceUrl.ts). (2) 20260925230845_messaging_closes_both_ways_off_job.sql adds is_off_job(job, user), an internal predicate: the user applied or was offered the job and is no longer a party (not the poster, not hired, no pending offer, not on the crew, no live application). - [~] **Q407** (OWNER DECISIONS, group + recurring jobs, owner 2026-09-25, answered in the session; these settle Q394, Q395 and the recurring lane's owner questions): GROUP: (1) a crew has NO lead: every hired member is equal in access (address, messaging the poster), pay share, cancellation-fee share and review; (2) a late poster cancellation's fee is split EVENLY across the hired crew; (3) one review PER Helpr, each counting toward that Helpr's own score and tier. RECURRING: (4) the poster chooses AT POSTING TIME between 'one person for every visit' and 'OK to split the days'; (5) when split, the first hired Helpr picks the visit dates they want, the poster then offers the remaining dates to the next Helpr they choose, and so on until every date is covered; a date nobody has picked stays offerable while the poster keeps offering it, and if it is still unfilled when it arrives it is not charged; (6) a Helpr giving up one of their dates (or ending their part of a series) gets a reliability strike ONLY within 24 hours of that visit, same as a late single-job cancel, and the date goes back to the poster to offer again; (7) a poster with a running series CANNOT delete their account until the series is ended; (8) after hire, a one-time job's date/time can change only as a request the Helpr accepts; (9) banning an account ends every series it posts or works on (future visits cancelled and not charged, the other party told). Build: group redesign on top of the reviewed roster-departure rework (group-fix 212b6db40); recurring split-days on top of recurring-fix. Each ships with a class guard and a money/authz review. **GROUP (1)-(3) BUILT 2026-09-25 on branch group-fix, NOT deployed, NOT pushed (migration 20260925154606_group_crew_has_no_lead + edge + client):** jobs.helper_id is NULL on every group job, enforced in every context by trg_group_job_has_no_lead (backfill keeps any former lead on the roster); helpers_needed / is_group_job locked once funded or hired (money review HIGH-1); each member's share frozen in cents at hire (group_job_helpers.slot_no / share_cents, largest remainder: $100/3 = 3334+3333+3333, MEDIUM-3) and paid from it by process-scheduled-payouts; every hire needs a funded job; proof photos: every member uploads to and reads the job folder, written to their own roster row through rpc_group_member_set_proof (PhotoProof crew path, HelperPhotoAsk reads the member's own slot); completion/cancel/payout notices go to every member; cancel_escrow re-reads the roster after its claim and pages on a stuck claim; an under-filled crew completes when every HIRED member is done and the unfilled shares are refunded to the poster (rule crew_completes_when_hired_done(), default true, owner to confirm, MEDIUM-4); tips: manual tip names a roster member (ReviewForm's 5-star prompt tips the reviewed member), auto-tip splits the poster's auto-tip across the crew (one claim per member); money-reconciliation grades a crew member by member (crew_transfers_exceed_escrow, crew_member_unpaid_on_released_job, per-member tier). FOUND AND FIXED on the way: accept_group_application's status CASE assigned text to the job_status enum, so every crew hire since 20260804122000 raised (pglite R0; not checked live). GUARDS: src/test/groupCrewNoLead.test.ts (class guard: the invariant, an exact two-way inventory of 14 helper_id-keyed functions that grant nothing on a crew, the hire, owner rules SQL==TS, fee ledger, reviews, proof photos, tips, cancel_escrow; 16 @mutate, all red), src/test/crewShareAllocation.test.ts (every budget $0-$3,000 x N=1..8 sums exactly; 2 @mutate), src/test/pglite/groupCrewNoLead.pglite.mjs (R0-R5 red on the before state, A1-A38 green, applied 3x), src/test/edge/void-cancelled-payments-crew.test.ts, process-scheduled-payouts-crew.test.ts, money-reconciliation-crew.test.ts, create-payment.test.ts (crew cancel_escrow + crew tip). OPEN: two owner rules to confirm (crew_fee_pays_unconfirmed, crew_completes_when_hired_done); the follow-ups in Q709 and Q710; screenshots before/after of the poster's completed crew card (Review chip, no Tip chip), the Cancel dialog on a crew job (fee lines), the post-form crew split line, and a crew member's On-site/Working card (Before/After Photo chips) — group jobs are behind GROUP_JOBS_ENABLED=false, so none is reachable in prod until the flag flips; NEEDS a REVIEW-ONLY lh-money-escrow + lh-authz-rls pass on 19d40e3ca..HEAD. To verify on prod after db-deploy: see the prod queries in the lane report (pg_get_functiondef/proacl of the restated functions, pg_trigger for trg_group_job_has_no_lead / trg_freeze_crew_member_share, pg_constraint reviews_one_per_reviewee_per_job, pg_indexes tips_one_auto_per_job_member, `SELECT count(*) FROM jobs WHERE is_group_job AND helper_id IS NOT NULL` = 0). ADDENDUM (owner, 2026-09-25, answered in the session): (10) on a SPLIT series a permanent ban of ONE Helpr hands back only that Helpr's dates to the poster to offer again (the other Helprs keep theirs; a handed-back date still unfilled when it arrives is not charged); a permanent ban of the POSTER still ends the whole series; a TEMPORARY ban pauses the series (confirmed); (11) a Helpr cancelling a ONE-TIME job gets a reliability strike ONLY within 24h of the start, the same rule as series visits; (12) the platform NEVER absorbs a fee: a refund of an already-charged visit (unfilled, ban-ended) returns the amount paid minus Stripe's processing fee, never more than the platform received net; (13) group jobs, CORRECTED by the owner the same day: a late-cancel fee is shared by every crew member once the poster has OFFERED them the job (hired), whether or not they confirmed (crew_fee_pays_unconfirmed() = true, as built); an under-filled crew completes and the unfilled shares are refunded (confirmed). The owner wants a detailed review later of 'once offered vs once confirmed' across ALL fees (Q706); (14) messaging closes BOTH ways once someone is off a job (rejected applicant, removed crew member, declined/expired offeree): neither side can start new messages on that job, old ones stay readable; (15) confirmed as built: on a split series the first hired Helpr has visit one; never-picked dates are offered by the poster, only given-up dates are open to the series' other Helprs to claim.

- [ ] **Q446** (OWNER/DEVICE, OA-018 identity linking, 2026-09-25; supersedes Q359's steps): what an existing email account gets when it signs in with Apple/Google is now settled from GoTrue's source (supabase/auth linking.go + external.go, read 2026-09-25) and checked on our DB layer by `scripts/check-identity-linking.mjs` (db-smoke.yml on every migration PR, db-drift-detect.yml nightly on prod, rolled back): verified same email -> the SAME account (no second profile, profile data kept); UNverified email account -> the same account, GoTrue wipes its password and confirms the email (profile kept); Apple "Hide My Email" -> a NEW account that lands on /complete-profile; an unverified provider email -> refused with provider_email_needs_verification, now explained on /login (was "That page needs an account": ~/.lh-shots/oa018/before-375.png vs after-375.png). What no script can do is a real consent screen. Steps, each on web (Chrome) AND the iOS TestFlight build, with an address you control: (1) sign up with email+password, verify it, post one job; sign out; tap Continue with Google with the same Gmail -> you must land in the SAME account (the job is there; admin: `select user_id, provider from auth.identities where user_id = '<id>'` shows email + google). (2) Same with Sign in with Apple choosing "Share My Email" -> same account. (3) Sign in with Apple choosing "Hide My Email" -> a NEW account on the Complete your profile form (expected; see Q447). (4) Sign up with email+password and do NOT click the verify link; then Continue with Google on that address -> you are signed in to that account, and the password you set no longer works (Forgot Password restores it; see Q447). Record user ids and screenshots in the OA-018 note. Close with the four results, not before.
- [ ] **Q447** (OWNER DECISION, OA-018 follow-ups, 2026-09-25, from GoTrue's source, not reproduced on a device): (a) PRE-ACCOUNT DATA, **MEDIUM** (rated by the lh-authz-rls review of #1806, 2026-09-26): when an UNverified email account is linked (Q446 step 4), GoTrue removes its password and email identity to stop a pre-account takeover, but OUR profile row (name, phone, DOB, photo, city, bio, marketing consent, legal acceptances, referral) is kept as whoever typed it at signup. Wider than first listed (code read 2026-09-26): complete-signup's unauthenticated path (supabase/functions/complete-signup/index.ts ~200-269: account under 30 min old, never signed in, empty bio) also takes the avatar, license and insurance uploads, writes legal_acceptances and records the referral, and because it fills every ProtectedRoute gate field the victim never passes /complete-profile to see it; the attacker's phone becomes the contact shown to counterparties. GoTrue leaves the attacker no session, refresh token, MFA factor or phone identity (token.go refuses the password grant to an unconfirmed email; signup refuses email+phone together), and cleanup-abandoned-accounts deletes unconfirmed accounts after 30 days. Referral attribution should also require a confirmed email. If an attacker signed up first with a victim's address, the victim's Google sign-in lands in an account whose profile the attacker wrote. Options: keep (today), or clear the signup-entered profile fields when email_confirmed_at is first set by a provider link (no email identity left), sending the person back through /complete-profile. Clearing also costs a legitimate user who signed up and then used Google instead of the email link. (b) APPLE HIDE-MY-EMAIL makes a second account for someone who already has one; /complete-profile could say "Already have a Helpr account under another email? Sign out and log in with it" when the address is @privaterelay.appleid.com. Guard to name when done: a case in scripts/sql/identity-linking-scenarios.sql for (a); a /complete-profile test for (b).

(a) The capture needs the pending marker and the redirect path: a refusal still falls through to "That page needs an account" when sessionStorage is blocked, when the attempt is older than 15 min, or when GoTrue sends the error to the Site URL instead of /home because redirect_to is not allow-listed (GoTrue validates redirect_to against site_url's host and uri_allow_list; neither is in supabase/config.toml and I could not read prod's). To close: measure CLS on a real Android Chrome with Bodoni throttled; if it is over 0.02, add a "Bodoni Moda Fallback Noto" family (local("Noto Serif"), metrics by the method in src/index.css's fallback comment, measured against NotoSerif-{Regular,Bold,BoldItalic}) after the Liberation one, and extend src/test/heroFontSwapDoesNotShift.test.ts to require it.

Candidates by code shape only (renders <EmptyState> and branches on isLoading): src/pages/jobs/JobDetail.tsx, src/pages/user/UserProfile.tsx, src/pages/profile/HomeHistory.tsx, StrSettings.tsx, PetProfiles.tsx, GiftCard.tsx, HelperAnalytics.tsx, src/components/admin/marketing/MarketingQueue.tsx; arrival-gate callers src/components/profile/SecurityTab.tsx and EarningsTab.tsx. To close: for each, reproduce it the way Q332 was (hold the primary request, go offline, read the screen at +10 s), move the ones that show a false empty state onto src/lib/feedPhase.ts, and add a repo-wide guard (no `useArrivalGate(!isLoading` / empty-state-on-`!isLoading` over a useQuery result) once the list is empty.
- [ ] **Q572** (LOW, found working Q349, 2026-09-26, measured once): listing a Storage bucket as anon fails with an ERROR instead of a clean empty/denied answer. `POST /storage/v1/object/list/avatars` with the publishable key returned 403 `{"code":"AccessDenied","message":"permission denied for table jobs"}`, so a storage.objects SELECT policy reads public.jobs, which anon has no grant on. The refusal itself is correct (anon should not list avatars); the problem is that it comes from a permission error inside a policy rather than the policy's own condition, so any anon storage read that reaches that policy errors. Not read: pg_policies for storage.objects (no service role here). To close: find the policy with `select policyname, qual from pg_policies where schemaname='storage' and tablename='objects' and qual ilike '%jobs%'`, and scope it `TO authenticated` (or guard the jobs read) so anon gets a plain deny; add it to the anon-grant class guard.

[]`, i.e. "nobody is blocked", so jobs from people the viewer blocked (or who blocked them) show in the feed until the next good read. getBlockedUserIds (nav badge, inbox) already fails closed; the feed does not. Not measured live. To close: make a `user_blocks` error fail the context query (the feed's existing error state) or hide the feed rather than show unfiltered jobs, and add it to src/test/userBlocksSharedRead.test.ts or a feed test.

- [~] **Q511** (lh-authz-rls review of Q335, 2026-09-26; pre-existing, not introduced by Q335): a Restore on one device was undone by another — unarchiveConversation deleted the server row, but a second device's local mirror still held the key and its next loadArchives merge-up pushed it back, hiding the thread everywhere. Fixed 2026-09-26 (owner: "fix Q511 too", branch cloud/open-messaging-deleted): archivedConversations keeps a per-user PENDING set (safeStorage `helpr_archived_conversations_pending_<uid>`) of archives this device made and never saw confirmed; archiveConversation marks pending and clears on a confirmed write (or rollback), unarchiveConversation clears it, and the merge-up (pure `planMergeUp`) pushes only pending keys and DROPS a local-only key the server confirmed once and no longer has. Upgrade note: a device's first load after this ships has no pending set, so any archive it made offline and never synced is dropped once (the thread reappears and can be archived again) — chosen over re-pushing, which is the bug. Guard: src/test/mirrorMergeUpPushesOnlyPending.test.ts (class = every src/lib module with a safeStorage mirror and an .upsert merge-up, derived from source; KNOWN_PUSHES_ALL exact both ways; 4 @mutate lines, each shown red). Left: live two-device check (restore on device B, reload device A, thread stays in the inbox) — not possible from this session (no test-account credentials).

- [ ] **Q550** (found by Q326, 2026-09-26): a11y-webkit-prod.yml's sweep job signs in as the shared test accounts but does not hold the `prod-lifecycle-shared-accounts` job lock. Its two-engine matrix (chromium + webkit side by side for the diff) is why: one lock would serialise the engines, and GitHub's one-pending-per-group rule could cancel the queued engine. Its workflow group is prod-load on every trigger, so it never overlaps a scheduled suite or a press run, but a DISPATCHED split suite (journeys, prod-audit, slow-network, loading-states, privacy) can still overlap it. Fix: run both engines inside one job that holds the lock, as press-every-control now does (scripts/audit/press-wave.sh), then take its dispatch out of prod-load and remove it from DISPATCH_SHARES_GROUP. Guard to update: src/test/sharedAccountJobsHoldOneLock.test.ts (delete its NOT_LOCKED entry; the test fails until you do).
- [ ] **Q551** (found by Q326, 2026-09-26): vacuity.yml's vacuity job holds shared-account credentials without the account lock. It runs on every push, and in the lock each push's guard proof would queue behind a 5-hour press run and be cancelled by the next push. It signs in only when a registered e2e guard's files changed, but then it drives the shared accounts beside whatever else is running. Fix options: move the credentialed (e2e) registrations into a separate job that holds the lock and runs only when such a registration is selected; or make those registrations use a dedicated seed account no other suite drives. Guard to update: src/test/sharedAccountJobsHoldOneLock.test.ts (its NOT_LOCKED entry).

REVIEW (lh-money-escrow, review-only, 2026-09-26): safe to ship; its MEDIUM finding fixed: a job already handled stays in the 24h scan and counts as a disposition every run, so the rule alone pages only when EVERY row fails (prod 2026-09-26 had 3 seed stuck jobs already logged in the window). detect_stuck_payments' per-row catch now files each failed row through log_cron_defect (source detect_stuck_payments, or detect_stuck_payments-seed for a seed job) instead of RAISE NOTICE only; sweep_release_last_chance's outer handler reports pushed 0 (it rolls the run back); the key-contract guard reads keys from RETURN objects only. CLASS GUARD src/test/sqlCronFailuresAreFiled.test.ts: every error handler in a function a recorded SQL cron runs files what it catches, or is in KNOWN_SWALLOWS (8 functions, 10 handlers, each with its reason; exact, two-way); red on the pre-fix body and on its @mutate. Still not covered: a scan that fails before its first row reads as found 0 to the rule (log_cron_defect files it, capped hourly); already_alerted is deduped per poster, not per job (old behaviour). CAP (2026-09-26, owner ask): log_cron_defect's hourly cap (20 rows per function) is no longer silent: migration 20260926043528_log_cron_defect_cap_alerts files ONE 'defect-cap' error_logs row per function per hour on the first drop and counts every later drop in its context.dropped (same source, so a '-seed' function stays out of paging; the cap row does not count toward the cap). GUARD src/test/logCronDefectCapIsLoud.test.ts (both @mutate proven red); PGlite src/test/pglite/logCronDefectCapAlerts.pglite.mjs (applied 3x, ALL PASS; the previous body filed 20 of 25 failures and no trace of the other 5).

Each PR's CI checks it against the main it branched from, not against the other PRs landing with it. Fix direction: a merge-queue check (or a pre-push full vitest on the combined tree). - [x] **Q717** (sensitive-review gate, 2026-09-26, owner approved the edit 09-26): the gate judged its window by the committer's LOCAL date and `git log --since` in the machine's zone, so this Mac reported 0 unreviewed while CI found 2. Fixed: `START_DATE` is the instant the gate landed (`2026-09-26T06:01:59Z`), `windowStart()` makes a bare date 00:00Z, and `audit()` compares `Date.parse` instants (scripts/lib/sensitiveReview.mjs). Guard: `src/test/sensitiveReview.test.ts` "the window is judged in UTC, whatever offset the committer had" — red before the fix (1 of 11 failed), green after (11/11). Re-measured: `--range origin/main --strict` gives the same 6 commits / 3 sensitive / 3 unreviewed under TZ=UTC, America/Los_Angeles and Asia/Tokyo; those 3 (bbc626beb, 42a7962cc, 39451a470) are now reviewed in docs/reviews/sensitive-reviews.jsonl.
